Let's detail `_lse_optimize`:

```
def _lse_optimize(p, stop_time, max_iter=300, beta_factor=None, seed=0):
    n=p.size
    if n<=1: return p
    p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return p
    p=p/s
    M = np.convolve(p,p).max()
    if beta_factor is None: beta = max(1.0, 30.0/max(M,1e-12))
    def objective(u, return_grad=True):
       theta=np.empty(n); theta[0]=0; theta[1:]=u
       m=theta.max()
       e=np.exp(theta-m)
       p=e/e.sum()
       b=np.convolve(p,p)
       x=beta*b
       mx=x.max()
       ex=np.exp(x-mx)
       Z=ex.sum()
       val=(mx+np.log(Z))/beta
       if not return_grad: return val
       lam=ex/Z
       h=np.correlate(lam,p,mode='valid')
       gp=2.0*h/beta
       mean=np.dot(p,gp)
       gth=p*(gp-mean)
       # add small reg? val += 1e-12*np.dot(u,u)
       return val, gth[1:]
    init = np.log(p+1e-300)
    init[0]=0
    # if init has nonfinite, set 0
    lo = init - 20? We need bounds relative: maybe (-20,20) not enough if init log ratios beyond. Use bounds = [( -init_abs? )]. Since gauge, ratios could be large if p very skewed. Use global bounds (-40,40) but if init outside, clip. If p has very uneven, clipped changes. Could instead set bounds around init ±10: lower=init-10, upper=init+10? For each variable, that restricts total relative changes exp(10) ~22000, maybe okay but could be too tight for global. Use fixed (-30,30) and clip init to bounds.
    bounds=[(-30,30)]*(n-1)
    res=minimize(objective, init clipped, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':..., 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20, 'maxcor':20})
```

```
def _convolve_fft(p):
  N=next_fast_len(2*p.size-1)
  F=rfft(p,N)
  return irfft(F*F,N)[:2*p.size-1]

def _grad_from_weights(p, lam):
  # lam length 2n-1
  revp = p[::-1]
  # full convolution lam * revp length (2n-1)+n-1=3n-2
  N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
  L=rfft(lam,N); P=rfft(revp,N)
  conv=irfft(L*P,N)
  h=conv[n-1:n+n-1]
  return h
```

```
def _lp_response(p, M, ctx, tlimit=1.0):
```

```
  for it in range(max_iters):

    b=conv(p,p); M=b.max()
    if score<p... update best
    # response for factors
    improved=False
    # maybe pick factors adaptively: [1+1e-9, 1-1e-4, 1-5e-4,1-1e-3]
    for f in factors:
      Mlp=M*f
      if Mlp<=0: continue
      q=lpr ...
      if q is None: continue
      if all q <=1e-8: continue
      # if q close to p, skip
      if np.linalg.norm(q-p) < 1e-5: continue
      r= line_search(p,q)
      if r improved: p=r; improved=True; break
    if improved: continue
    # fallback soft gradient direction
    r= _soft_gradient_step(p, beta_scale=30)
    if r improved: p=r; improved=True; no_improve=0; continue
    # if stuck, random perturbation
    no_improve++
    if no_improve>20: p = norm(p*exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n))); no_improve=0
```

Soft gradient step implementation (exact line search) for fallback:
```
def _soft_step(p, beta=None):
  b=conv(p,p); M=b.max()
  if beta is None: beta=30/max(M,1e-12)
  x=beta*b; mx=x.max(); ex=np.exp(x-mx); lam=ex/ex.sum()
  h=correlate(lam,p,'valid')
  g=2*h
  # direction for log: c = g - g.mean()? We want p_new = p*exp(step*(mean(g)-g))
  c = g - np.mean(g)
  scale = np.std(c)
  if scale < 1e-14: return None
  c = -c/scale  # high g reduced
  # initial step candidates: 0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0 maybe
  cur=score
  best_t=None
  for step in [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]:
     r=p*np.exp(step*c)
     s=r.sum(); if s<=0 or not finite: continue
     r/=s
     sc=score(r)
     if sc<cur*(1-1e-12): best=r...
  # refine around best step using bounded? Could use 5 trials.
```
Since step dimensionless; if c scaled by std, step 0.1 causes multiplicative exp ±0.1, moderate. Can improve. But might be slow. Use only few.

KKT method details:
`_kkt_polish(p, stop_time, max_iters=2000, eta_factors?)`:
```
n=p.size
p=norm; best=p; best_M=conv_max(p)
ctx: idx=np.add.outer(np.arange(n),np.arange(n)) # shape n,n values 0..2n-2
lam = _weights_from_b(p, eta=30)
for it in range(max_iters):
  if time>stop: break
  T=lam[idx]
  try:
    res=lsq_linear(T, np.ones(n), bounds=(0.0, None), lsmr? ) # options maybe
    a=res.x
    a_norm=a.sum(); if a_norm>0: a/=a_norm
    else: break
  except: break
  # maybe clip small extras? a nonneg by bounds.
  # blend line search p to a
  r, m = _line_search_blend(p,a, current_M=..., ts=[...])
  if r is not None and m < best_M*(1-1e-12):
     best=p? set p=r; best_M=m; continuation
  else:
     # try reverse direction? a maybe bad. Try small t=0.1? No improve.
     # update lam anyway? Could be helpful.
  # update lam from current p (not necessarily new) using eta maybe adaptive
  b=conv(p,p); lam=_weights_from_b(b, eta)
  # optional perturbation of lam if stuck
```

```
def _lse_optimize(p, stop, max_iters=300, beta_factor=20.0):
   n=p.size; M=conv_max(p); beta=max(1.0,beta_factor/max(M,1e-12))
   ref=np.argmax(p)
   var_idx=np.array([i for i in range(n) if i!=ref])
   logp=np.log(p+1e-300)
   init=logp[1:]?? Need map.
   u_init=np.clip(logp[var_idx]-logp[ref], -B, B)
   state={'best_p':p.copy(),'best_m':M,'last_b':None,'count':0}
   def f(u, return_grad=True):
       theta=np.empty(n); theta[ref]=0; theta[var_idx]=u
       # clip u? bounds should keep, but in objective during line search maybe within.
       mth=np.max(theta); e=np.exp(theta-mth); pnew=e/e.sum()
       b=_conv(pnew)  # direct
       x=beta*b; mx=x.max(); ex=np.exp(x-mx); Z=ex.sum(); val=(mx+logZ)/beta
       state['last_b']=b
       # update best? maybe every call
       mval=b.max(); if mval < state['best_m']*(1-1e-13): state['best_m']=mval; state['best_p']=pnew.copy()
       if not return_grad: return val
       lam=ex/Z
       h=np.correlate(lam,pnew,mode='valid')
       gp=2*h/beta
       mean=np.dot(pnew,gp)
       gt=pnew*(gp-mean)
       return val, gt[var_idx]
   def callback(kk):
       if time.monotonic() > stop: return True
       if kk % 10 ==0 and state['last_b'] is not None:
          # state best updated in f
          pass
       return time.monotonic() > stop
   try: res=minimize(f, u_init, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=[(-B,B)]*len(var_idx), options={'maxiter':max_iters,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':20,'maxcor':10})
   except: ...
   _update best with state best and final p from u
   return state best
```

Quick length resampling details:
```
def _resample(p, m):
  n0=p.size
  if m==n0: return p.copy()
  if m<2: return p.copy()
  xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n0)
  xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
  q=np.interp(xn,xo,p)
  q=np.clip(q,0,1000)
  s=q.sum(); if s<...: q=np.ones(m)/m
  else q=q/s
  return q
```

Should we implement FFT for large? To be safe, implement both. But need ensure correctness. Let's implement direct for n<=600, FFT for >600. For n=600, direct 720k, okay. Quick lengths up to 1024 may use FFT if threshold 512? Direct 1M okay; but many quick evals maybe. Let's implement FFT threshold 400 to be faster. Need verify FFT convolution.
```
def _conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    m=2*n-1
    if n <= 300:
        return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=next_fast_len(m)
    F=rfft(a,N)
    out=irfft(F*F,N)
    out = out[:m]
    # small negative noise
    # for max, negative negligible
    return out
```
For score max, if negative tiny, max unaffected because positive. For constraints in KKT using conv max only. For LSE weights, b could have tiny negative for far constraints; exp(beta*b) with negative okay. If b negative tiny -1e-17, x lower; fine. If due FFT significant negative? unlikely with N enough. Use direct for n<=512 for reliability. For n>512, FFT.
Gradient `np.correlate` for LSE if n<=512 direct; for n>512 maybe implement FFT. But LSE for n>512 maybe optional. Let's implement FFT correlate for n>300 to be safe. Need ensure correct. We'll test mentally with small arrays? Let's derive carefully.
Want h_j = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} p_i λ_{j+i}, j=0..n-1. λ length L=2n-1.
Define revp = p[::-1] length n. Full conv c = λ * revp (length L+n-1=3n-2). c[m] = ∑_{k=0}^{L-1} λ_k revp_{m-k} where revp index 0..n-1.
Set m = j + n - 1. Then c[j+n-1] = ∑_{k} λ_k p_{n-1-(k-(j+n-1))}? Let i = n-1 - (m-k)? Let q=m-k, revp_q=p_{n-1-q}. Need q=n-1-i -> k=m-(n-1-i)=j+n-1-n+1+i=j+i. Good. p_i. Sum i such that k in [0,L-1], q in [0,n-1]. For m=j+n-1, as i ranges 0..n-1, k=j+i ranges j..j+n-1, within 0..2n-2 because j≤n-1 => max2n-2, min0. Good. So c[j+n-1]=∑ p_i λ_{j+i}. Thus h = c[n-1:n+n-1] (slice start n-1 length n). Good.
FFT convolution: Need N>=L+n-1=3n-2. Use `rfft(λ,N) * rfft(revp,N)`, irfft. It returns circular conv length N since N enough. Floating noise. Good.
Use direct `np.correlate(λ,p,'valid')` for n<=300. This might be simpler; for n>300 use FFT.

```
def _kkt_polish(p, stop_time, max_iters=5000, seed=0):
    n=p.size
    if n<4 or n>400: return p
    p=_norm(p); if p is None return p
    idx = np.add.outer(np.arange(n), np.arange(n))  # This can be large 512^2=262k, fine.
    ones=np.ones(n)
    eta_cycle=[10,30,100,300]
    best=p; bestM=convMax
    for cycle in cycles:
       if time>stop: break
       eta=...
       b=conv(p,p); lam=weights(b,eta)
       stuck=0
       sub_stop = min(stop_time, time+ max(10,budget?)) # maybe run until stop for each cycle? Better allocate total time equally. Simpler: while time<stop and iters<max per eta.
```

```
etas=[10,30,100,300]; ei=0
for it in range(max_iters):
   if time>stop: break
   eta=etas[ei % len(etas)]
   ...
   if no improvement for 50: ei=(ei+1)%len; stuck=0; # maybe perturb lam
```

```
def _as_array(x):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    if arr.size==0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all(): return None
    return arr
```

`_line_search_line` with max conv:
```
def _line_search_line(p,q, current_M, refine=True, stop_time=None, max_extra=0):
    if p is None or q is None: return None,current_M
    p=np.asarray(p); q=np.asarray(q)
    # ensure sum1? maybe if abs(sum-1)>1e-6 normalize both
    if abs(p.sum()-1)>1e-5: p=_norm(p)
    if abs(q.sum()-1)>1e-5: q=_norm(q)
    ts=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.45,0.6,0.8,1.0)
    best_t=0.0; best_m=current_M
    for t in ts:
       if stop_time is not None and time.monotonic()>stop_time: break
       r=(1.0-t)*p + t*q
       m=_convMaxNorm(r)
       if m < best_m - 1e-15: best_m=m; best_t=t
    if best_t <=0: return None,current_M
    if refine:
       lo=max(0.0, best_t-0.15); hi=min(1.0, best_t+0.15)
       if best_t==1.0:
          lo=0.5; hi=1.0
       elif best_t==0.05:
          lo=0.0; hi=min(1,best_t+0.15)
       for _ in range(12):
          if stop_time and time>stop: break
          if hi-lo<1e-4: break
          mid=(lo+hi)/2
          r=(1-mid)*p+mid*q
          m=_convMaxNorm(r)
          if m < best_m - 1e-15:
             best_m=m; best_t=mid; hi=mid
          else:
             lo=mid
    r=(1-best_t)*p+best_t*q
    return _norm(r), best_m
```
The binary assumes function decreases then increases; if best_t at t=1, lo=0.5 hi=1. If best at low 0.05, hi 0.2. Good.
But if best_m equal current? We use m < best_m - 1e-15, so tiny no.

`_soft_current_steps` fallback:
```
def _soft_attempt(p, beta_factor=30.0, try_improve_only=True):
   b=_conv(p); M=b.max()
   if M<=0: return p
   lam=_weights_from_b(b,beta_factor)
   h=_weighted_shift_sum(p,lam)
   g=2.0*h
   c=g-np.mean(g)
   scale=np.std(c)
   if scale<1e-14: return p
   best=p; best_M=M
   for step in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
       r=p*np.exp(-step*c/scale)
       r=_norm(r)
       if r is None: continue
       m=_convMaxNorm(r)
       if m<best_M: best=r; best_M=m
   return best
```
This returns best among exponential tilts. Use in LP stuck? It does not line search blend; okay. It may be slow (6 conv per call). Use sparingly.

Weights:
```
def _weights_from_b(b, eta):
   b=np.asarray(b,float); b=np.maximum(b,0)
   M=b.max()
   if M<=0 or not np.isfinite(M): return np.ones(b.size)/b.size
   x=eta*b/M
   x=x-np.max(x)
   ex=np.exp(x)
   s=ex.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones... 
   return ex/s
```

```
def _kkt_polish(p, stop_time, max_iters=1000, seed=0):
   n=p.size
   if n<4 or n>350: return p
   p=_norm(p)
   if p is None: return p
   best=p.copy(); best_M=_convMaxNorm(p)
   idx = np.add.outer(np.arange(n), np.arange(n))
   ones=np.ones(n)
   etas=[10.0,30.0,100.0,300.0,1000.0]
   ei=0; stuck=0
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic()>=stop_time: break
      eta=etas[ei % len(etas)]
      b=_conv(p); M=b.max()
      if M<best_M: best_M=M; best=p.copy()
      lam=_weights_from_b(b,eta)
      T=lam[idx]
      try:
         res=lsq_linear(T, ones, bounds=(0.0, 10.0), method='trf')
         a=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
      except Exception:
         a=None
      if a is None: continue
      a=_norm(a)
      if a is None: continue
      # try blend
      r,m=_line_search_line(p,a,M,refine=False,stop_time=stop_time)
      accepted=False
      if r is not None and m < M*(1-1e-12):
         p=r; accepted=True
      else:
         # if a directly good, maybe set to a? line search includes t=1, so if not, no.
         ma=_convMaxNorm(a)
         if ma < M*1.05:
            p=_norm(0.6*p+0.4*a)
            accepted=True
      if accepted:
         stuck=0
      else:
         stuck +=1
         if stuck > 20:
            # perturb p or change eta
            p=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.03,n)); p=_norm(p); stuck=0; ei=(ei+1)%len(etas)
         elif stuck % 5 ==0:
            ei=(ei+1)%len(etas)
      if time.monotonic() < stop_time:
         # ensure best updated
         pm=_convMaxNorm(p)
         if pm < best_M: best_M=pm; best=p.copy()
   return best
```
`lsq_linear` may be deterministic; no seed. Good.

LP context:
```
def _LPContext(n):
   m=2*n-1
   ks=[]; js=[]; vidx=[]
   for k in range(m):
      lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
      if lo<=hi:
         js.extend(range(lo,hi+1))
         ks.extend([k]*(hi-lo+1))
         # vidx = k-j for j=lo..hi => k-lo down to k-hi
         vidx.extend(range(k-lo, k-hi-1, -1))
   return (np.array(ks,int), np.array(js,int), np.array(vidx,int), m)

def _build_A(p, ctx):
 ks,js,vidx,m=ctx
 A=np.zeros((m,p.size), float)
 A[ks,js]=p[vidx]
 return A
```
Check vidx order: For k=3,n=4, lo=0,hi=3? Actually hi=min(3,3)=3, lo=0. j=0,1,2,3; k-j=3,2,1,0. range(k-lo=3, k-hi-1=-1, -1) gives 3,2,1,0 good. For k=1: lo=0,hi=1 -> range(1, -? k-hi-1= -? 1-1-1=-1) gives1,0. good.

LP solve:
```
def _lp_solve(A, n, M, tlimit):
   c=-np.ones(n)
   b=np.full(A.shape[0], M)
   opts={'time_limit': tlimit, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'range':? no}
   try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options=opts)
   except Exception:
      try: res=linprog(... options={})
      except: return None
   if res.status !=0: return None
   x=res.x
   if x is None: return None
   x=np.asarray(x,float); x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
   s=x.sum()
   if s <1e-12 or not finite: return None
   return x/s
```
Maybe if M very small, x=0 status optimum but sum0; return None.

LP polish:
```
def _lp_polish(p, stop_time, max_iters=10000, seed=0, n_max=300):
  p=_norm(p); if p None return
  n=p.size
  if n<4 or n>n_max: return p
  ctx=_LPContext(n)
  best=p; best_M=conv
  rng=default_rng
  stuck=0; iter=0
  factors_cycle? We'll adapt.
  A=None
  for iter in range(max_iters):
    if time>stop: break
    b=_conv(p); M=b.max()
    if M<best_M: best=p.copy(); best_M=M; stuck=0
    else: stuck+=1
    # build A (depends p) each iter
    try: A=_build_A(p,ctx)
    except: break
    tried=False
    # factors: start [1+1e-9]; if stuck >=5 append 1-1e-4, 1-5e-4, 1-1e-3; if stuck>=20 append 0.98,0.95
    factors=[1.0+1e-9]
    if stuck>=5: factors.extend([1.0-1e-4,1.0-5e-4])
    if stuck>=15: factors.extend([1.0-1e-3,0.98])
    if stuck>=40: factors.extend([0.95])
    tlimit = max(0.3, min(2.0, (stop_time - time.monotonic())*0.2))
    for f in factors:
       if time>stop: break
       Mlp=M*f
       if Mlp<=0: continue
       q=_lp_solve(A,n,Mlp,tlimit)
       if q is None: continue
       if np.linalg.norm(q-p, ord=1) < 1e-5: continue
       r,m=_line_search_line(p,q,M,refine=True,stop_time=stop_time)
       if r is not None and m < M*(1-1e-12):
          p=r; tried=True; stuck=0; break
    if tried: continue
    # try soft step
    p_soft=_soft_attempt(p, beta_factor=30)
    msoft=convMax(p_soft)
    if msoft < M*(1-1e-12):
       p=p_soft; stuck=0; continue
    # perturb
    if stuck > 30:
       p=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)); p=_norm(p); stuck=0
    else:
       # try small random direction
       d=rng.normal(size=n); d-=d.mean(); p=_norm(p+0.02*d); stuck+=1
  return best
```
This may be expensive due line search refine each LP. But okay. If LP returns q close p often, stuck increments; perturb.
Maybe use `np.linalg.norm(q-p, ord=1)` could be small due same distribution; threshold 1e-4.
Factors with lower M may give q with sum feasible small; normalized maybe odd. Line search might not improve. but exploration.

Time in LP: If `tlimit` large but many solves, could exceed. `tlimit` based on remaining*0.2; for 200s remaining, 40s too high? min 2.0 sets 2. Good. Use max 1.0. Set tlimit=1.0. If Highs presolve, okay.

Potential issue: Highs `time_limit` in seconds, if iteration remaining small but tlimit 1, safe. But if budget small, stop check.

LSE optimize code with ref mapping:
```
def _lse_optimize(p, stop_time, beta_factor=30.0, max_iters=400):
  p=_norm(p); if p None return
  n=p.size
  if n<4: return p
  M=_convMaxNorm(p)
  beta=max(1.0, beta_factor/max(M,1e-12))
  # if beta too large? x max beta_factor, okay.
  ref=int(np.argmax(p))
  var_idx=np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != ref], dtype=int)
  logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
  u0=np.clip(logp[var_idx]-logp[ref], -50, 50)
  B=50.0
  best=p.copy(); best_M=M
  state={'last_p':None,'last_b':None,'count':0}
  def fun(u, return_grad=True):
     theta=np.empty(n,float)
     theta[ref]=0.0
     theta[var_idx]=u
     # If u has nan? return large
     if not np.isfinite(theta).all():
        if return_grad:
           return 1e100, np.zeros_like(u)
        return 1e100
     mt=np.max(theta)
     e=np.exp(theta-mt)
     pnew=e/e.sum()
     if not np.isfinite(pnew).all() or pnew.sum()<=0:
        pnew=p.copy()
     b=_conv(pnew)
     b=np.maximum(b,0)
     x=beta*b
     mx=np.max(x)
     ex=np.exp(x-mx)
     Z=np.sum(ex)
     val = (2.0*n)*(mx + np.log(max(Z,1e-300)))/beta
     state['last_p']=pnew; state['last_b']=b
```

```
def _quick_phase(base, stop, rng):
    base=base.copy(); best=base; best_sc=_score_exact(base)
    pool=[(best_sc, base.copy())]
    def consider(p): nonlocal best,best_sc
       p=_norm(p); if None return
       sc=_score_exact(p); if finite and sc<best_sc: best=p.copy(); best_sc=sc; pool.append((sc,p.copy()))
    n0=base.size
    lengths=set()
    for d in list(range(-8,9)) + [-15,-20,-30,15,20,30,50]:
       nt=n0+d; if 16<=nt<=1500: lengths.add(nt)
    for nt in [192,200,204,210,220,240,256,320,384,512,768,1024]: if... add
    for nt in lengths:
       if time.monotonic()>stop: break
       q=_resample(base, nt); consider(q)
       q2=_resample_full(base, nt)? Maybe one method enough. Could try centers full. Let's implement `_resample` with centers by default; and `_resample2` with endpoint? Might double. Keep one.
    # transposed? reverse same.
    # log perturbations
    for sigma in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
       for _ in range(3):
          if time>stop: break
          q=base*np.exp(sigma*rng.normal(size=n0)); consider(q)
    # oscillatory perturbations
    x=np.arange(n0)/float(n0)
    modes=[1,2,3,4,5,6,8,10]
    for amp in (0.1,0.25,0.5):
       for m in modes:
          if time>stop: break
          for ph in (0.0, np.pi/2):
             v=amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
             q=base*(1.0+v)
             q=np.maximum(q,1e-8); consider(q)
             q2=np.maximum(1.0+v,1e-8) # uniform base? maybe
             consider(q2)
    # prune base
    for frac in (1e-10,1e-8,1e-6): q=base; q[q<frac*q.max()]=0; consider(q)
    # sort pool
    pool.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    # deduplicate? Not necessary.
    return best, pool
```

```
def _resample(p, m):

 if m<1: return p
 xo=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
 xo_now = xo
 xn=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
 q=np.interp(xn, xo, p)
 q=np.clip(q,0,None); s=q.sum(); if s<... q=np.ones(m)/m else q=q/s
 return q
```

Helper `_fft_conv(a)`:
```
def _fft_conv(a):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    N=next_fast_len(m)
    F=rfft(a,N)
    return irfft(F*F,N)[:m]
```
For n=1024, N=2048. Good.

`_fft_correlate(lam,p)`:
```
def _fft_correlate(lam,p):
   n=p.size; L=lam.size
   N=next_fast_len(L+n-1)
   A=rfft(lam,N); B=rfft(p[::-1],N)
   c=irfft(A*B,N)
   h=c[n-1:n+n-1]
   if h.size<n: # pad
      h=np.pad(h,(0,n-h.size))
   return h
```
Direct correlate for small. `_weighted_shift_sum`:
```
def _weighted_shift_sum(p,lam):
   n=p.size
   if n <= 256:
      h=np.correlate(lam, p, mode='valid')
   else:
      h=_fft_correlate(lam,p)
   return h
```
Check `np.correlate` order: It returns `c[k] = sum_n a_{n+k} v_n`. If a=lam, v=p, good. If p length > lam? lam always longer. Good.

`_convMaxNorm`:
```
def _convMaxNorm(p):
   c=_conv(p)
   m=float(c.max(initial=0.0)) if c.size else 0.0
   return max(0.0, m)
```

`_score_exact`:
```
def _score_exact(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   if p.size==0: return inf
   p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)? Better check finite.
   if not np.isfinite(p).all(): return inf
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   s=float(p.sum())
   if s<0.01: return inf
   c=np.convolve(p,p)
   m=float(c.max())
   if not np.isfinite(m) or m<0: m=0.0
   return float(2*p.size*m/(s*s))
```
Evaluator clips after nan; if p inf returns inf. We return finite.

```
s=arr.sum()
if s<1e-12: return None
arr=arr/s
if not np.isfinite(arr).all(): return None
return arr
```

Let's write `_lse_optimize` in code carefully with nonlocal:
```
def _lse_optimize(p, stop_time, beta_factor=30.0, max_iters=400):
    p=_normalize(p)
    if p is None: return p
    n=p.size
    if n < 4: return p
    scale=2.0*n
    M=_convMaxNorm(p)
    beta=max(1.0, float(beta_factor)/max(M, 1e-12))
    # If beta too huge? x range beta_factor, okay.
    ref=int(np.argmax(p))
    var_idx=np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != ref], dtype=int)
    logp=np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    u0=np.clip(logp[var_idx]-logp[ref], -50.0, 50.0)
    B=50.0
    best=p.copy(); best_M=M
    def objective(u):
        nonlocal best, best_M
        theta=np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        theta[ref]=0.0
        theta[var_idx]=u
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=float)
        mt=theta.max()
        e=np.exp(theta-mt)
        # if sum underflow? e has at least 1 at max; sum>0
        ss=e.sum()
        if ss <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
            pn=p.copy()
        else:
            pn=e/ss
        b=_conv(pn)
        b=np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        x=beta*b
        mx=x.max()
        ex=np.exp(x-mx)
        Z=ex.sum()
        if Z <= 0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
            val=1e100
            grad=np.zeros_like(u)
            return val, grad
        val=scale*(mx + np.log(Z))/beta
        m=float(b.max())
        if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M=m; best=pn.copy()
        lam=ex/Z
        h=_weighted_shift_sum(pn, lam)
        gp=(2.0*h)/beta
        mean=float(np.dot(pn, gp))
        gr=pn*(gp-mean)
        gr=gr*scale
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gr)):
            grad=np.zeros_like(u)
        else:
            grad=gr[var_idx]
        return float(val), grad
    def callback(kk):
        return time.monotonic() > stop_time
    try:
        res=minimize(objective, u0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                     bounds=[(-B,B)]*len(var_idx),
                     options={'maxiter':int(max_iters), 'ftol':1e-11, 'gtol':1e-8,
                              'maxls':20, 'maxcor':10},
                     callback=callback)
    except Exception:
        res=None
    if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
        u=res.x
        theta=np.empty(n); theta[ref]=0; theta[var_idx]=u
        mt=theta.max(); e=np.exp(theta-mt); s=e.sum()
        if s>0:
           pn=e/s; _ update best? Since objective did, but final maybe.
           m=float(_conv(pn).max()); if finite and m<best_M: best=pn.copy(); best_M=m
    # prune
    for frac in (1e-12,1e-10,1e-8):
        q=best.copy(); qmax=q.max()
        if qmax>0:
           q[q < frac*qmax]=0.0
           q=_normalize(q)
           if q is not None:
              m=float(np.convolve(q,q).max())  # exact for update? Use direct maybe.
              if m*2*n < best_M*2*n? Since sum1, m compare. But if exact sum maybe1. Use m.
              if m < best_M: best=q.copy(); best_M=m
    return best
```
But in prune, using direct `np.convolve` for n>1024 maybe heavy but rare. Use `_convMaxNorm`? For final exact update main will validate. For internal prune, use fast.

```
def _length_phase(base, stop, rng):
    best=base.copy(); best_sc=_score_exact(base)
    def upd(q): nonlocal best,best_sc ...
    n0=base.size
    lens=set()
    for d in list(range(-8,9)) + [-15,-20,-30,10,15,20,30,50]:
       nt=n0+d; if 16<=nt<=1200: lens.add(nt)
    for nt in [192,200,205,210,220,240,256,320,384,512,768,1024]: if... lens.add
    quick=[]
    for nt in sorted(lens, key=lambda x: abs(x-n0)):
       if time>stop: break
       q=_resample(base,nt); sc=_score_exact(q); if finite quick.append((sc,q,nt)); upd(q)
    if not quick: return best
    quick.sort()
    seen=set()
    for sc,q,nt in quick:
       if nt in seen or len(seen)>=4: continue
       seen.add(nt)
       if time>stop-60: break
       # kkt if nt<=350
       if nt<=350:
          qk=_kkt_polish(q, min(stop, time.monotonic()+40), max_iters=200)
          upd(qk)
          if nt<=280:
             ql=_lp_polish(q, min(stop, time.monotonic()+45), max_iters=800)
             upd(ql)
       # lse
       maxit = 200 if nt<=300 else (100 if nt<=512 else 50)
       per = 80 if nt<=300 else (50 if nt<=512 else 25)
       remaining = max(0.0, stop - time.monotonic())
       run_stop = min(stop, time.monotonic() + min(per, remaining*0.8))
       qlse=_lse_optimize(q, run_stop, beta_factor=80.0, max_iters=maxit)
       upd(qlse)
    return best
```
This may optimize up to 4 lengths. If quick already found better large length, q is that. Good.

Let's produce final code block. But before final, let's scrutinize possible mistakes in LSE gradient scaling.

Objective base: L = (1/β) log ∑ exp(β b_k). Scale S=2n. val=S*L.
Derivative wrt p: ∂L/∂p_j = (1/β) ∑_k ∂b_k/∂p_j exp_term = (1/β)*2∑λ_k p_{k-j} = (2/β) h_j.
Then dp/dθ: p_j(δ - p). grad θ_j = p_j(g_j - ∑p_i g_i). Multiply S. So gr=S*p*(gp - mean), gp=2h/β. Code does that. Good.

One nuance: The convolution b_k uses p*p; derivative ∂b_k/∂p_j = p_{k-j} + p_{k-j}? If k-j valid. If p_j appears twice when j = k-j (k even)? The formula still 2p_{k-j}. Yes. Good.

KKT condition: Tλ a constant; our T a_i = ∑_j λ_{i+j} a_j. Derivative h_i=∑λ_k p_{k-i}; with k=i+j => p_j λ_{i+j}. Yes. Good.

Now, check `_weighted_shift_sum` direct correlate: output h[k]=∑_i lam[k+i] p_i. Good.

Now, potential mismatch between `lam` length and direct correlate if λ has shape (2n-1,), p length n. valid length n. Good.

Now, in LSE objective, if `b` has tiny negative (FFT), `x=beta*b` after `b=np.maximum(b,0)`. Good. But if negative from FFT for constraints that should be small positive, setting to0 okay; weights slightly off. For n>256 only.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
try:
    from scipy.optimize import lsq_linear
except Exception:
    lsq_linear = None

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
except Exception:
    rfft = None
    irfft = None
    next_fast_len = None


_DIRECT_LIMIT = 256
_LP_N_MAX = 280
_KKT_N_MAX = 350
_TARGET = 1.50274349

_lp_ctx_cache = {}
_kkt_idx_cache = {}


def _as_finite_array(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    return a


def _parent_sequence():
    a = None
    try:
        a = _as_finite_array(globals().get("height_sequence_1"))
    except Exception:
        a = None
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(201, dtype=np.float64)
        a /= a.sum()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.sum() <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(201, dtype=np.float64)
        a /= a.sum()
    return a / a.sum()


def _normalize(p):
    if p is None:
        return None
    a = _as_finite_array(p)
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    a = a / s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    return a


def _fft_conv(a):
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_fast_len(m)
    F = rfft(a, N)
    return irfft(F * F, N)[:m]


def _conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size <= _DIRECT_LIMIT or rfft is None:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    return _fft_conv(a)


def _fft_shifted(p, lam):
    n = p.size
    L = lam.size
    N = next_fast_len(L + n - 1)
    A = rfft(lam, N)
    B = rfft(p[::-1], N)
    c = irfft(A * B, N)
    h = c[n - 1:n + n - 1]
    if h.size < n:
        h = np.pad(h, (0, n - h.size), mode="constant")
    return h[:n]


def _weighted_shift_sum(p, lam):
    n = p.size
    if n <= _DIRECT_LIMIT or rfft is None:
        return np.correlate(lam, p, mode="valid")[:n]
    return _fft_shifted(p, lam)


def _conv_max_norm(p):
    c = _conv(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 0.0
    return max(0.0, m)


def _score_exact(p):
    a = _as_finite_array(p)
    if a is None or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _weights_from_b(b, eta):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    M = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(b.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(b.size)
    x = float(eta) * b / M
    x = x - x.max()
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(b.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(b.size)
    return ex / s


def _line_search_line(p, q, current_M, refine=True, stop_time=None):
    if p is None or q is None:
        return None, float(current_M)
    p = _normalize(p)
    q = _normalize(q)
    if p is None or q is None:
        return None, float(current_M)
    n = p.size
    cur = float(current_M)
    ts = (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.45, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0)
    best_t = 0.0
    best_m = cur

    for t in ts:
        if stop_time is not None and time.monotonic() > stop_time:
            break
        r = (1.0 - float(t)) * p + float(t) * q
        m = _conv_max_norm(r)
        if m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best_t = float(t)

    if best_t <= 0.0:
        return None, cur

    if refine:
        lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.15)
        hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.15)
        if best_t >= 0.999:
            lo = 0.5
            hi = 1.0
        elif best_t <= 0.06:
            lo = 0.0
            hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.15)
        for _ in range(12):
            if stop_time is not None and time.monotonic() > stop_time:
                break
            if hi - lo < 1e-4:
                break
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            r = (1.0 - mid) * p + mid * q
            m = _conv_max_norm(r)
            if m < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m = m
                best_t = mid
                hi = mid
            else:
                lo = mid

    r = (1.0 - best_t) * p + best_t * q
    return _normalize(r), best_m


def _soft_attempt(p, beta_factor=30.0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = p.size
    b = _conv(p)
    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
        return p
    lam = _weights_from_b(b, beta_factor)
    h = _weighted_shift_sum(p, lam)
    g = 2.0 * h
    c = g - float(np.mean(g))
    sc = float(np.std(c))
    if sc <= 1e-14:
        return p
    best = p.copy()
    best_m = M
    for step in (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0):
        r = p * np.exp(-float(step) * c / sc)
        r = _normalize(r)
        if r is None:
            continue
        m = _conv_max_norm(r)
        if m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best = r.copy()
    return best


def _get_lp_ctx(n):
    if n in _lp_ctx_cache:
        return _lp_ctx_cache[n]
    m = 2 * n - 1
    ks = []
    js = []
    vid = []
    for k in range(m):
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if lo <= hi:
            js.extend(range(lo, hi + 1))
            ks.extend([k] * (hi - lo + 1))
            vid.extend(range(k - lo, k - hi - 1, -1))
    ctx = (
        np.asarray(ks, dtype=np.intp),
        np.asarray(js, dtype=np.intp),
        np.asarray(vid, dtype=np.intp),
        m,
    )
    _lp_ctx_cache[n] = ctx
    return ctx


def _build_lp_A(p, ctx):
    ks, js, vid, m = ctx
    A = np.zeros((m, p.size), dtype=np.float64)
    if ks.size:
        A[ks, js] = p[vid]
    return A


def _lp_solve(A, n, M, tlimit, bounds):
    if M is None or not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(A.shape[0], float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        opts = {
            "time_limit": float(tlimit),
            "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
            "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
        }
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None:
        return None
    if getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0:
        return None
    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None
    x = _normalize(x)
    if x is None:
        return None
    if float(x.max()) <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return x


def _lp_polish(p, stop_time, max_iters=10000, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = p.size
    if n < 6 or n > _LP_N_MAX:
        return p

    ctx = _get_lp_ctx(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    best = p.copy()
    best_M = _conv_max_norm(best)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stuck = 0

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
            break

        b = _conv(p)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
            M = 1e-12

        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best = p.copy()
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        try:
            A = _build_lp_A(p, ctx)
        except Exception:
            break

        remaining = max(0.0, stop_time - time.monotonic())
        if remaining <= 0.25:
            break
        tlimit = max(0.25, min(1.0, 0.2 * remaining))

        improved = False
        factors = [1.0 + 1e-9]
        if stuck >= 4:
            factors.extend([1.0 - 1e-4, 1.0 - 5e-4])
        if stuck >= 12:
            factors.extend([1.0 - 1e-3, 0.98])
        if stuck >= 35:
            factors.extend([0.95])

        for f in factors:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
                break
            q = _lp_solve(A, n, M * f, tlimit, bounds)
            if q is None:
                continue
            if float(np.linalg.norm(q - p, ord=1)) < 1e-6:
                continue
            r, m = _line_search_line(p, q, M, refine=True, stop_time=stop_time)
            if r is not None and m < M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                p = r
                improved = True
                stuck = 0
                break

        if improved:
            continue

        ps = _soft_attempt(p, beta_factor=40.0)
        ms = _conv_max_norm(ps)
        if ps is not None and ms < M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            p = ps
            stuck = 0
            continue

        if stuck % 7 == 0:
            step = 0.02 + 0.003 * min(stuck // 7, 8)
            d = rng.normal(size=n)
            d -= d.mean()
            p = _normalize(p + step * d)
            if p is None:
                break
        elif stuck >= 30:
            p = _normalize(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)))
            if p is None:
                break
            stuck = 0

    return best


def _get_kkt_idx(n):
    if n in _kkt_idx_cache:
        return _kkt_idx_cache[n]
    idx = np.add.outer(np.arange(n), np.arange(n)).astype(np.intp)
    _kkt_idx_cache[n] = idx
    return idx


def _kkt_polish(p, stop_time, max_iters=900, seed=0):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    if p.size < 6 or p.size > _KKT_N_MAX or lsq_linear is None:
        return p

    n = p.size
    idx = _get_kkt_idx(n)
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    best = p.copy()
    best_M = _conv_max_norm(best)
    etas = (10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0, 1000.0)
    ei = 0
    stuck = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
            break

        M = _conv_max_norm(p)
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best = p.copy()

        b = _conv(p)
        lam = _weights_from_b(b, etas[ei % len(etas)])
        T = lam[idx]

        try:
            res = lsq_linear(T, ones, bounds=(0.0, 10.0), verbose=0)
            a = _normalize(res.x)
        except Exception:
            a = None

        if a is None:
            continue

        r, m = _line_search_line(p, a, M, refine=False, stop_time=stop_time)
        accepted = False
        if r is not None and m < M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            p = r
            accepted = True
        else:
            ma = _conv_max_norm(a)
            if ma < M * 1.04:
                p = _normalize(0.6 * p + 0.4 * a)
                if p is not None:
                    accepted = True

        if accepted:
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1
            if stuck >= 25:
                p = _normalize(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)))
                if p is None:
                    break
                stuck = 0
            ei += 1

        pm = _conv_max_norm(p)
        if pm < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = pm
            best = p.copy()

    return best


def _lse_optimize(p, stop_time, beta_factor=30.0, max_iters=400):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = p.size
    if n < 6:
        return p

    scale = 2.0 * n
    M = _conv_max_norm(p)
    beta = max(1.0, float(beta_factor) / max(M, 1e-12))

    if n > 512:
        max_iters = min(int(max_iters), 70)
    elif n > 300:
        max_iters = min(int(max_iters), 160)

    ref = int(np.argmax(p))
    var_idx = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != ref], dtype=int)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    u0 = np.clip(logp[var_idx] - logp[ref], -50.0, 50.0)
    B = 50.0

    best = p.copy()
    best_M = M

    def objective(u):
        nonlocal best, best_M
        theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        theta[ref] = 0.0
        theta[var_idx] = u
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)

        mt = float(theta.max())
        e = np.exp(theta - mt)
        ss = float(e.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ss):
            pn = p.copy()
        else:
            pn = e / ss

        b = _conv(pn)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        x = beta * b
        mx = float(x.max())
        ex = np.exp(x - mx)
        Z = float(ex.sum())
        if Z <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(Z):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)

        val = scale * (mx + float(np.log(Z))) / beta
        m = float(b.max())
        if np.isfinite(m) and m < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = m
            best = pn.copy()

        lam = ex / Z
        h = _weighted_shift_sum(pn, lam)
        gp = (2.0 * h) / beta
        mean = float(np.dot(pn, gp))
        gr = scale * pn * (gp - mean)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gr)):
            grad = np.zeros_like(u, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            grad = gr[var_idx]
        return float(val), grad

    def callback(kk):
        return time.monotonic() > stop_time

    try:
        res = minimize(
            objective,
            u0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=[(-B, B)] * len(var_idx),
            options={
                "maxiter": int(max_iters),
                "ftol": 1e-11,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 20,
                "maxcor": 10,
            },
            callback=callback,
        )
    except Exception:
        res = None

    if res is not None:
        u = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if u is not None:
            theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            theta[ref] = 0.0
            theta[var_idx] = u
            mt = float(theta.max())
            e = np.exp(theta - mt)
            ss = float(e.sum())
            if ss > 1e-300:
                pn = _normalize(e / ss)
                if pn is not None:
                    m = _conv_max_norm(pn)
                    if m < best_M - 1e-15:
                        best_M = m
                        best = pn.copy()

    qm = best.max()
    if qm > 0:
        for frac in (1e-12, 1e-10, 1e-8):
            q = best.copy()
            q[q < frac * qm] = 0.0
            q = _normalize(q)
            if q is not None:
                mq = _conv_max_norm(q)
                if mq < best_M - 1e-15:
                    best_M = mq
                    best = q.copy()

    return best


def _resample(p, m):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = p.size
    m = int(m)
    if m < 1:
        m = 1
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    if m == n:
        return p.copy()

    xo = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    xn = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    q = np.interp(xn, xo, p)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
    q = _normalize(q)
    if q is None:
        q = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return q


def _quick_phase(base, stop_time, rng):
    base = _normalize(base)
    if base is None:
        return None, []
    best = base.copy()
    best_sc = _score_exact(base)
    pool = [(best_sc, base.copy())]

    def consider(p):
        nonlocal best, best_sc
        q = _normalize(p)
        if q is None:
            return
        sc = _score_exact(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = q.copy()
            pool.append((sc, q.copy()))

    n0 = base.size
    lengths = set()
    for d in list(range(-8, 9)) + [-15, -20, -30, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50]:
        nt = n0 + d
        if 16 <= nt <= 1200:
            lengths.add(nt)
    for nt in (192, 200, 204, 210, 220, 240, 256, 320, 384, 512, 768, 1024):
        if 16 <= nt <= 1200:
            lengths.add(nt)

    for nt in sorted(lengths, key=lambda x: abs(x - n0)):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
            break
        q = _resample(base, nt)
        if q is not None:
            consider(q)

    for sigma in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40):
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
                break
            q = base * np.exp(float(sigma) * rng.normal(size=n0))
            consider(q)

    x = np.arange(n0, dtype=np.float64) / float(n0)
    modes = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 10)
    for amp in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
        for m in modes:
            if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
                break
            for ph in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi):
                v = float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
                consider(np.maximum(base * (1.0 + v), 1e-8))
                consider(np.maximum(1.0 + v, 1e-8))

    qm = base.max()
    if qm > 0:
        for frac in (1e-12, 1e-10, 1e-8, 1e-6):
            q = base.copy()
            q[q < frac * qm] = 0.0
            consider(q)

    pool.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    return best, pool


def _length_phase(base, stop_time, rng):
    base = _normalize(base)
    if base is None:
        return None
    best = base.copy()
    best_sc = _score_exact(base)

    def consider(p):
        nonlocal best, best_sc
        q = _normalize(p)
        if q is None:
            return
        sc = _score_exact(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = q.copy()

    n0 = base.size
    lengths = set()
    for d in list(range(-8, 9)) + [-15, -20, -30, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50]:
        nt = n0 + d
        if 16 <= nt <= 1200:
            lengths.add(nt)
    for nt in (192, 200, 205, 210, 220, 240, 256, 320, 384, 512, 768, 1024):
        if 16 <= nt <= 1200:
            lengths.add(nt)

    quick = []
    for nt in sorted(lengths, key=lambda x: abs(x - n0)):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
            break
        q = _resample(base, nt)
        if q is None:
            continue
        sc = _score_exact(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            quick.append((sc, q, nt))
        consider(q)

    if not quick:
        return best

    quick.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    seen = set()
    for _, q, nt in quick:
        if nt in seen or len(seen) >= 4:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time - 45.0:
            break
        seen.add(nt)

        if nt <= _KKT_N_MAX:
            stop1 = min(stop_time, time.monotonic() + 35.0)
            if time.monotonic() < stop1:
                qk = _kkt_polish(q, stop1, max_iters=220, seed=17)
                consider(qk)

        if nt <= _LP_N_MAX:
            stop2 = min(stop_time, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
            if time.monotonic() < stop2:
                ql = _lp_polish(q, stop2, max_iters=1200, seed=23)
                consider(ql)

        if nt <= 300:
            run_stop = min(stop_time, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
            maxit = 220
        elif nt <= 512:
            run_stop = min(stop_time, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
            maxit = 110
        else:
            run_stop = min(stop_time, time.monotonic() + 22.0)
            maxit = 55

        if time.monotonic() < run_stop:
            qs = _lse_optimize(q, run_stop, beta_factor=80.0, max_iters=maxit)
            consider(qs)

    return best


def _postprocess(p):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_exact(p)
    qm = best.max()
    if qm > 0:
        for frac in (1e-14, 1e-12, 1e-10, 1e-8, 1e-6):
            q = best.copy()
            q[q < frac * qm] = 0.0
            q = _normalize(q)
            if q is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_exact(q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = q.copy()
    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    hard = t0 + max(20.0, budget - 25.0)

    stops = {}
    stops["quick"] = t0 + max(8.0, min(45.0, 0.05 * budget))
    stops["top"] = t0 + max(stops["quick"] + 4.0, min(110.0, 0.12 * budget))
    stops["kkt"] = t0 + max(stops["top"] + 4.0, min(270.0, 0.25 * budget))
    stops["lp"] = t0 + max(stops["kkt"] + 4.0, min(580.0, 0.50 * budget))
    stops["lse"] = t0 + max(stops["lp"] + 4.0, min(1020.0, 0.80 * budget))
    stops["len"] = t0 + max(stops["lse"] + 4.0, budget - 120.0)
    stops["final"] = t0 + max(stops["len"] + 4.0, budget - 30.0)
    for k in list(stops.keys()):
        stops[k] = min(float(stops[k]), float(hard))

    p0 = _parent_sequence()
    best_p = _normalize(p0)
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(201, dtype=np.float64) / 201.0
    best_sc = _score_exact(best_p)

    def update(p):
        nonlocal best_p, best_sc
        q = _normalize(p)
        if q is None:
            return
        sc = _score_exact(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = q.copy()

    update(best_p)

    # Quick candidate/length scan.
    if time.monotonic() < stops["quick"] + 1.0:
        qb, pool = _quick_phase(best_p, stops["quick"], rng)
        update(qb)
    else:
        pool = []

    # Short local optimization from a few attractive quick candidates.
    if pool and time.monotonic() < stops["top"]:
        for sc, p in pool[:4]:
            if time.monotonic() > stops["top"]:
                break
            if p is None or p.size > 700:
                continue
            if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > best_sc * 1.03 + 1e-12:
                continue
            run_stop = min(stops["top"], time.monotonic() + 18.0)
            q = _lse_optimize(p, run_stop, beta_factor=70.0, max_iters=120 if p.size <= 300 else 60)
            update(q)

    # KKT linear-response polish.
    if time.monotonic() < stops["kkt"]:
        q = _kkt_polish(best_p, stops["kkt"], max_iters=900, seed=101)
        update(q)

    # LP best-response polish for moderate sizes.
    if best_p.size <= _LP_N_MAX and time.monotonic() < stops["lp"]:
        q = _lp_polish(best_p, stops["lp"], max_iters=25000, seed=202)
        update(q)

    # Smooth LSE anneal / polish.
    if time.monotonic() < stops["lse"]:
        for bf, mx in ((20.0, 420), (80.0, 360), (250.0, 300)):
            rem = max(0.0, stops["lse"] - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 8.0:
                break
            n = best_p.size
            if n <= 200:
                run_stop = min(stops["lse"], time.monotonic() + min(mx, 0.85 * rem))
                mi = mx
            elif n <= 400:
                run_stop = min(stops["lse"], time.monotonic() + min(int(0.6 * mx), 0.7 * rem))
                mi = int(0.6 * mx)
            else:
                run_stop = min(stops["lse"], time.monotonic() + min(int(0.35 * mx), 0.7 * rem))
                mi = int(0.35 * mx)
            if run_stop > time.monotonic():
                q = _lse_optimize(best_p, run_stop, beta_factor=bf, max_iters=mi)
                update(q)

    # Length/resolution phase.
    if time.monotonic() < stops["len"] - 30.0:
        q = _length_phase(best_p, stops["len"], rng)
        update(q)

    # Final polish.
    if best_p.size <= _LP_N_MAX and time.monotonic() < stops["final"] - 25.0:
        q = _lp_polish(best_p, min(stops["final"] - 20.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0),
                       max_iters=5000, seed=999)
        update(q)

    if time.monotonic() < stops["final"] - 8.0:
        n = best_p.size
        mx = 320 if n <= 220 else (160 if n <= 450 else 70)
        run_stop = min(stops["final"] - 3.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
        q = _lse_optimize(best_p, run_stop, beta_factor=150.0, max_iters=mx)
        update(q)

    final_p = _postprocess(best_p)
    if final_p is None:
        final_p = best_p
    update(final_p)

    final_p = _normalize(final_p)
    if final_p is None:
        final_p = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64)
        final_p /= 32.0

    out = [float(x) for x in final_p]

    try:
        fs = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        fs = _score_exact(final_p)

    if not np.isfinite(fs):
        fallback = [1.0] * 32
        try:
            fsc = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback))
        except Exception:
            fsc = _score_exact(np.array(fallback))
        if np.isfinite(fsc):
            return fallback
        return [1.0]

    return out
```
